- **think-model-first**: 思维模型：建模先于代码。写碰到状态与操作的代码之前，先把每个操作写成偏函数，写明它在哪些前置状态下有定义；再全化，定义域之外的每一格给一个确定的答复，不变式排除的格写不可达；最后翻成领域模型与对象模型再写代码，每个类型能指回模型。设计或实现会话、任务、连接、资源这类有生灭的对象，加一个操作或一个状态，或实现里出现「不该发生」的分支时加载；人说「先建模」「全化一下」「状态表呢」，或点名 /think-model-first 时也加载。不碰状态的纯计算与纯文档改动不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-model-first) · ★1)

- **think-orthogonal**: 思维模型：正交、分形、矩阵。列清单、拆设计、划边界或定位一类反复出现的问题之前，先拆出独立变化的轴，每条轴归一个拥有者；把轴交叉成表逐格看，每格要么有答案要么写明不适用；再问这个结构在大一层和小一层是不是同一个，是就只设计一次。凭感觉列清单、同一个判断在两处各写一遍、一个概念的状态在决定另一个领域的事、特例越补越多时加载；人说「先找轴」「拆成矩阵」「这是同一个结构」，或点名 /think-orthogonal 时也加载。只有一个量在变、没有可拆的不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-orthogonal) · ★1)
- **think-ought-vs-is**: 思维模型：应然与实然。做设计、实现、优化、重构、定位问题，或准备把一件事交给人定之前，先答三问：它应该是什么样，它现在是什么样，两者差在哪；差异就是全部工作。开始一项设计或一个切片、同一类问题第二次出现、一件事做得比预计久、准备重跑贵的命令找原因时加载；人说「先写应然」「本质解」「别缝缝补补」，或点名 /think-ought-vs-is 时也加载。只查一个已知事实不加载。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-ought-vs-is) · ★1)

- **think-through**: 議論・思考プロセスの作法を矯正するスキル。毎ターン適用する想定の常時注入型。 ユーザー発言を唯々諾々で受け取らず、事象から原因へ降り、提案を検証まで通す構造で考え抜く。 ロジックツリー上位から再帰的に問いを解き、TBD 暫定全体で構造合意を先取りし、 複数事項や作業中の状態変化では、未決事項に依存しない確定事項を先に完了してから残る未決事項を決める。 広くvariationのある対象では、具体caseと方針群を往復し、全caseを扱えるまで帰納する。 抽象と具体の往復で意味を曲解されない原則を立てる。 「議論する」「論点を整理する」「どう設計？」「どう実装？」「修正したい」「エラーが出た」 「選択肢を出す」「指摘を反映する」「型を更新する」など、思考プロセス・議論プロセスが絡む全場面で適用する。 steering / task-design のいずれを呼ぶ前段にも常に効く。 ([tumeda1992/ai_agent_dev_skill_plugin](https://github.com/tumeda1992/ai_agent_dev_skill_plugin/tree/HEAD/.steering/2026/202610/20261018-escalate-pending-plugin-improvements/artifacts/migration-baseline/plugins/tumeda-dev/skills/think-through) · 2 copies)

- **think-top3**: Produces a worked three-framework analysis of a topic. Does NOT trigger when the user has explicitly chosen the approach or library, when the task is under ~10 lines, when fixing a bug in existing code, or for infra/terraform/k8s and… ([albertobarnabo/lazy-cat](https://github.com/albertobarnabo/lazy-cat/tree/HEAD/skills/think-twice) · ★50)
- **think-twice**: Invoke before `git push`. Reactive coding runs on a silent confusion — the agent believes that saving itself one round-trip helps the user. It doesn't. When the skipped scan would have caught something, the agent saves itself 20 seconds and costs the user two hours. The account is inverted. This skill does exactly one thing — makes the agent see the inverted account in the second before push, not after CI catches it. Use whenever a change feels "quick", "small", or "obvious" — those are the moments where the inversion is most tempting and most expensive. ([xiaogeli/claude-think-twice](https://github.com/xiaogeli/claude-think-twice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/think-twice) · ★2)

- **think-twice**: Use when the user wants to start something new — a project, skill, feature, or ongoing commitment — or asks whether an idea is worth pursuing. - **think-unknowns**: 思维模型：不知为不知。任务没说清、一句话有两种读法或有不知道的事时，先过三句：先认出来并分清是事实、惯例还是偏好，事实去试，惯例去查，偏好归人；读法让一个没看过它的脑子只拿原文独立再读一遍；消不掉的两种都满足，或选限制更少的一种，收在一处并明说。把任务读成要求、全化表出现填不出的格、想到第二种读法、写下「大概」「应该是」、准备问人或没有人可问时加载；人说「别猜」「这句还能怎么读」「查一下惯例」，或点名 /think-unknowns 时也加载。任务写明了、只有一种读法不加载。证据算不算数归 think-evidence。 ([PanQiWei/pqw-skills](https://github.com/PanQiWei/pqw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/think-unknowns) · ★1)

- **thinking-moves**: 思考が詰まったときに、症状から適切な思考技法を選んで実際に回す。案が出ない・ 多すぎて選べない・同じ案ばかり・問いそのものが歪んでいる・全員がすぐ同意する・ 良さそうだが不安、といった詰まりに対し、発散/収束/再定義/批判のどれが要るかを 診断して実行する。トリガー: アイデア出して / 案を広げて / 発散させて / もっと選択肢 / 絞り込みたい / どれを選ぶべき / 決められない / 手詰まり / 行き詰まった / 同じ案しか 出ない / 問いが違う気がする / 観点が足りない / 抜け漏れない? / 前提を疑って / ブレスト / 壁打ち / 一緒に考えて / 発想を変えたい / SCAMPER / TRIZ / KJ法 / pre-mortem / プレモータム / 6-3-5 / 逆に考えて / brainstorm / ideate / diverge / narrow down / help me think / stuck on this。 「この設計どう思う?」のように評価を求められた場面でも、選択肢が1つしか 机上にないなら発散から入る価値がある。名指しされた技法(SCAMPER で、 pre-mortem やって)は正確な手順で回す。思考技法の一覧や説明を求められた ときにも使う。 ([tapoh22334/dotfiles](https://github.com/tapoh22334/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/config/claude/.claude/skills/thinking-moves))

- **thinking-suite**: 思维模型集合总入口：deep-thinking（结论前置层）、problem-definition（问题界定）、problem-analysis（系统分析）三个思考类 skill 的分工总览、路由决策、交接协议与歧义裁决。 当用户问"这几个思考 skill 什么区别 / 我该用哪个 / 帮我想清楚一件事但不知道用什么方法"，或抛出模糊的思考类请求需要路由判断时使用本 skill。 本 skill 不做界定、不做分析、不给结论。单场景请求直接找对应成员：判断题找 deep-thinking，问题没弄清找 problem-definition，系统分析找 problem-analysis。 Triggers when user mentions: - "思维模型集合 / 思考 skill 有什么区别 / 我该用哪个思考 skill" - "帮我想清楚一件事"（未指明界定还是分析时，本 skill 负责路由） - "界定问题和分析问题什么关系 / 这三个 skill 怎么分工" ([guangquan123/thinking-skills](https://github.com/guangquan123/thinking-skills/tree/HEAD/thinking-suite))
- **thinking-sunk-cost**: 使用沉没成本谬误思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：只看未来收益。核心：因已付出而做出非理性继续投入。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/cognitive-bias/sunk-cost/skills/sunk-cost) · ★4)
- **thinking-supply-demand**: 使用供需法则思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：市场分析基石。核心：价格由供需关系决定。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/systems/supply-demand/skills/supply-demand) · ★4)
- **thinking-survivorship-bias**: 使用幸存者偏差思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：寻找失败案例。核心：过度关注成功者，忽略沉默的失败。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/cognitive-bias/survivorship-bias/skills/survivorship-bias) · ★4)
- **thinking-swot**: 使用SWOT 分析思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：战略规划基础框架。核心：优势/劣势/机会/威胁。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/strategy/swot/skills/swot) · ★4)
- **thinking-systems**: When behavior is emergent across components—fixes elsewhere break, loops/delays dominate—map boundary, stocks/flows, feedback, archetypes, then rank leverage. ([tjboudreaux/cc-thinking-skills](https://github.com/tjboudreaux/cc-thinking-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-systems) · ★1.8k · 708 installs · 5 copies)

- **thinking-systems**: 使用系统思考思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：看清动态关系。核心：因果回路图，识别杠杆解。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/decision/systems/skills/systems) · ★4)

USE WHEN systems thinking, causal loop, feedback loops, archetypes, leverage points, iceberg model, fix the system, why does this keep happening, recurring problem, second-order effects. NOT FOR incident causal chains (use RootCauseAnalysis). ([johansenmeister/Dual-PAI](https://github.com/johansenmeister/Dual-PAI/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/thinking-systems-thinking) · ★2)
- **thinking-tech-adoption**: 使用技术采用生命周期思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：产品扩散规律。核心：创新者→早期采用者→早期大众→晚期大众→落后者。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/strategy/tech-adoption/skills/tech-adoption) · ★4)
- **thinking-theory-of-constraints**: When throughput or latency is pipeline-limited, identify the single binding constraint and exploit, subordinate, elevate, then recheck—ignore non-constraints. Use for architecture evaluation, edge case analysis, ethics considerations, and strategy development. - **thinking-threatmodel**: Tests ideas, decisions, and strategies across multiple time horizons and threat scenarios. Inspired by Daniel Miessler's WorldThreatModelHarness. [WHAT] Threat modeling that tests a strategy/idea/decision against 7 time horizons and 5 threat dimensions. Produces a vulnerability matrix and recommendations. [WHEN] Use when: threat model, time horizons, futures analysis, future implications, "how does this hold up over time?", "what happens if..." [EXPERTISE] Threat modeling, scenario analysis, temporal risk assessment, futures thinking ([carlheath/ogmios](https://github.com/carlheath/ogmios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/thinking-threatmodel) · ★9)
- **thinking-tipping-point**: 使用引爆点思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：流行与变革。核心：量变引发质变的关键门槛。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/systems/tipping-point/skills/tipping-point) · ★4)
- **thinking-to-recall**: Demonstrates that chain-of-thought reasoning improves LLM factual retrieval through computational buffering and self-priming. Improves single-hop factual accuracy by enabling models to generate contextual bridge facts before recalling answers. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/thinking-to-recall) · ★7)
- **thinking-toc**: 使用瓶颈理论(TOC)思维模型来分析问题、输出计划或输出文章。适用场景：识别并打破瓶颈。核心：系统强度由最弱一环决定。 ([OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace](https://github.com/OrangeCold/thinking-models-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/decision/toc/skills/toc) · ★4)
- **thinking-token-efficient**: use when a complex chat task needs adaptive private reasoning effort, token-efficient tool planning, code or artifact analysis, multi-step synthesis, evidence work, or validation without unnecessary reasoning. do not use for simple direct answers, ordinary rewriting or translation, visible prose compression, image generation, raw chain-of-thought disclosure, or shortcuts that reduce correctness, evidence, citations, validation, safety, compatibility, or required detail. ([ginmp8/rhapsodia](https://github.com/ginmp8/rhapsodia/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-token-efficient))
- **thinking-tool**: Use thinking tools (mcp_think or sequential thinking) to organize reasoning after context changes. ([irfanfaraaz/claude-kit](https://github.com/irfanfaraaz/claude-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/thinking-tool))
- **thinking-toolbox**: Use when the user is choosing what to work on, planning, brainstorming, weighing a decision, stuck, avoiding a task, overcommitted, relying on a surprising claim, handling a crisis or a tense group situation, building something for other people, trying to build a habit, or reviewing how they work. Covers Adam Jones's 38 thinking tools. ([mickzijdel/dev-hooks](https://github.com/mickzijdel/dev-hooks/tree/HEAD/plugins/thinking-tools/skills/thinking-toolbox))
- **thinking-toolkit**: 当用户评估项目想法或可行性、纠结技术选型或二选一方案、想深入研究某个公司/行业/股票/加密货币/期货标的、要核查某个市场说法或数据真伪、想法多但没行动需要最小成本试错、方案或架构被惯例卡死想推倒重来、想拆解优秀产品或竞品好在哪时使用。12种结构化思考方法工具箱(整理自卡兹克Prompt合集):苏格拉底式提问、双层解释、反向拆解、横纵分析、事实核查、专家会诊、第一性原理、跨领域借解、双向钢人论证、最小实验、天赋挖掘、人生设计。用户表达模糊困惑、想学习或研究某个事物、面临复杂难题犹豫不决、想探索天赋或人生方向,或直接点名以上任一方法时也适用。Triggers: 帮我把这个想法捋一捋，我总感觉哪里不对但说不上来；这个说法/这个数据是真的吗，帮我核查一下；我在两个方案之间犹豫很久了，帮我分析分析；我想系统研究一下这家公司/这个行业；看到一个好产品，帮我拆解它好在哪；想法很多但一直没行动，怎么小成本试一下；帮我找到我的天赋和优势；我对人生方向很迷茫，不知道接下来怎么走。 ([EtherealMingo/university-teacher-skills](https://github.com/EtherealMingo/university-teacher-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/learning-education/thinking-toolkit) · ★22)
- **thinking-toolkit**: Apply a named Thinking Toolkit model or audit an argument with /logic. Use only when the user explicitly invokes the toolkit, names an included model or alias, or invokes /logic. ([ponomr/thinking-toolkit](https://github.com/ponomr/thinking-toolkit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★22)
- **thinking-toolkit**: 思考决策工具箱元技能——整合 superforecaster（校准预测）、decision-matrix（决策矩阵）、 bayesian-reasoning（贝叶斯推理）、systems-thinking（系统思维）、scout-mindset-bias-check （认知偏见审查）、doctor-strange（平行宇宙沙盘推演）六大思考框架。 覆盖预测→决策→推理→系统分析→偏见审查→情景模拟的完整深度思考链路。 Trigger keywords: 思考工具, 决策工具, 深度思考, 预测, 决策矩阵, 贝叶斯推理, 系统思维, 偏见审查, 沙盘推演, thinking toolkit, superforecaster, decision matrix, bayesian reasoning, systems thinking, scout mindset, doctor strange, 怎么做决定, 帮我分析利弊, 预测一下, 模拟未来, 认知偏见, 理性决策。 ([ZhouYinLong-lab/Dog-Skills](https://github.com/ZhouYinLong-lab/Dog-Skills/tree/HEAD/thinking-toolkit) · ★7)
- **thinking-toolkit**: 当用户评估项目想法或可行性、纠结技术选型或二选一方案、想深入研究某个公司/行业/股票/加密货币/期货标的、要核查某个市场说法或数据真伪、想法多但没行动需要最小成本试错、方案或架构被惯例卡死想推倒重来、想拆解优秀产品或竞品好在哪时使用。12种结构化思考方法工具箱(整理自卡兹克Prompt合集):苏格拉底式提问、双层解释、反向拆解、横纵分析、事实核查、专家会诊、第一性原理、跨领域借解、双向钢人论证、最小实验、天赋挖掘、人生设计。用户表达模糊困惑、想学习或研究某个事物、面临复杂难题犹豫不决、想探索天赋或人生方向,或直接点名以上任一方法时也适用。 ([Rogers-F/thinking-toolkit](https://github.com/Rogers-F/thinking-toolkit/tree/HEAD/thinking-toolkit) · ★3)
- **thinking-tools**: Structured thinking patterns for agent self-reflection. - **thinking-tools**: Composable reasoning primitives for structured thinking. Use when facing a hard decision point, evaluating competing options, auditing assumptions, assessing risk, or any situation where "thinking harder" would help. Especially useful when injected into subagent prompts for implementation, review, or analysis tasks. Trigger on: thinking tools, reason through, think carefully, evaluate options, audit assumptions, pre-mortem, steelman, devil's advocate, red team, confidence check, decompose, or when dispatching agents that need structured reasoning. ([giannimassi/wits](https://github.com/giannimassi/wits/tree/HEAD/skills/think) · ★3)
- **thinking-tools**: Use when the user names a thinking tool, framework, or mental model, or asks for the diagram of one (for example Cynefin, issue tree, pre-mortem, evaporating cloud, decision matrix, decision tree, fishbone or Ishikawa diagram, causal loop diagram, OODA, Minto pyramid, Wardley map), asks which framework, method, or mental model to use, asks in so many words for a structured, systematic, or framework-based approach to a problem or decision, or asks for the agenda of a thinking, decision, or problem-solving workshop. - **thinking-tools**: Problem-framing toolkit. Use when the user brings a PROBLEM to think through (not a build/edit task) - it matches the problem's shape to a fitting framework (5 Whys, OODA, Eisenhower, SCAMPER, First Principles, ...) and applies it to turn vague input into usable data points.
